# Level flag lists

This reference page lists every world flag that the PS2 game's scripts place: 7,058 placements in 94 scripts. A
flag is a named point in a level with a heading; the game flags page (game-flags) says what flags are for. Every
value was read from the PS2 disc (NTSC-U, `SLUS_212.15`). The scripts are compiled Lua 4.0 chunks stored as entries
of `WARRIORS.WAD` and located through `WARRIORS.DIR` by the CRC-32 name hash described on the archive directory page.

## Disc sources

| Source | Scripts | Placements | Notes |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `level<N>.lua`, story and Coney levels | 29 | 5,395 | One script per level with a mission or free-roam map |
| `level<N>_<chapter>.lua` | 3 | 4 | `level20_marketplace`, `level93_chapter2`, `level95_homeless` |
| `level<N>_<mode>_init.lua`, Rumble arenas | 56 | 1,580 | The same 56 scripts as on the level list page |
| `kinghill.lua`, `royal.lua` | 2 | 4 | Rumble mode scripts shared by the arenas; all four positions are computed |
| Entries 6,932, 6,933, 7,105 and 10,537 | 4 | 75 | Scripts whose file names are not known; shown by entry and hash |

`WARRIORS.WAD` holds 467 compiled Lua chunks in all, every one with the same header (Lua 4.0, little-endian, 4-byte
integers and instructions, 8-byte numbers). The other 373 place no flags.

## Values per flag

Each placement gives five values, in the order of the columns below.

| Column | Values on the disc |
| --- | --- |
| Name | A string of 3 to 30 characters; 445 placements use a name longer than 15 characters |
| Position (x, y, z) | Three numbers, each written with at most one decimal place; 23 placements compute the position instead |
| Heading | A whole number from 0 to 360 |
| Activity | A whole number from 0 to 36; 0 in 4,906 placements |
| Group | A whole number from 0 to 97; 0 in 6,400 placements |

The unnamed entries are close to named ones: entry 6,932 places exactly the 25 flags of `level102_brawl_init.lua`
(entry 6,930), entry 6,933 shares 20 of its 22 flags with it, and entry 7,105 shares 23 of its 27 flags with
`level111_mercy_init.lua` (entry 7,106). Entry 10,537 places one flag, `fSWalk`, with a computed position.

## Flag room

Every level's scripts also set the level's pool sizes: two numbers and a list of four. The second number is the
level's flag room, shown as Room in the Scripts table. A chapter script shares its level's room (the value given is
the level script's). No level places more flags than its room: `level95.lua` and `level95_homeless.lua` together
place 395 against a room of 534, `level60.lua` to `level64.lua` place exactly their room, and 43 of the 56 Rumble
arena scripts place exactly their room. The mode scripts `kinghill.lua` and `royal.lua` set no room of their own.

## Scripts

One row per script that places flags, in the order of the flag lists below. Entry is the zero-based
index in `WARRIORS.DIR`; Room is the flag count the level's scripts declare for its flag pool (see
Flag room).

## Flag lists

Each list gives the flags in the order the script places them. A name placed twice appears twice.
Computed means the script works the position out while the level runs; it is not stored as
numbers.

## Acceptance checks

- `WARRIORS.WAD` holds 467 compiled Lua 4.0 chunks; 94 of them place flags, 7,058 placements in all.
- Entry 6,930 of `WARRIORS.DIR` is `level102_brawl_init.lua` (hash `0x26B8CDC8`, 4,163 bytes); it places 25 flags,
  the first `fP1_1` at -9.1, 8.9, -11.1 with heading 128, and sets a flag room of 25.
- `level95.lua` (entry 9,400, hash `0x4808DE63`) places 393 flags, more than any other script; `level11.lua`
  (entry 7,072) places 388.
- Every constant position on this page is written on the disc with at most one decimal place, and every heading is
  a whole number from 0 to 360.
- No level's scripts place more flags than the level's flag room.
- `level2.lua` places five flags named `Tag_01` to `Tag_05`, each with activity 12.
- All 323 placements with activity 33 are named `_xfHideZone` followed by a number.
- `level54.lua` places the name `_xfBumNet_11` six times; 44 names are placed more than once within one script.
- For all 56 Rumble arena scripts, the number of player-1 flags and the position and heading of the flag the level
  list page (ref-levels) names first agree with this page. `level121_brawl_init.lua` places `fP1_02` before
  `fP1_01`.

## Open questions

- The file names of entries 6,932, 6,933, 7,105 and 10,537, and whether the game ever runs them.
- The unit of the positions and the direction a heading of 0 faces (also open on the level list page).
- What each activity and group value does in game; the game flags page gives what the flag names suggest.
- The positions the 23 computed placements take while a level runs.
- The Xbox disc's copies of these scripts were not compared.
